The single equation is also the source of the Schrödinger equation, the Dirac equation with its full spinorial and Clifford structure, the Minkowski metric signature, the Lorentz invariance of c, special relativity, the Schwarzschild metric, the Einstein field equations, Newton&#8217;s law of universal gravitation, the Standard Model gauge group SU(3) × SU(2) × U(1), Maxwell&#8217;s equations, the Yang–Mills equations, the Higgs mechanism, CP and T violation and their CPT pairing, exact photon masslessness, integer charge quantization, the absence of magnetic monopoles, the absence of the spin-2 graviton, the second law of thermodynamics, the seven arrows of time unified, the Hubble expansion, the cosmological constant with no free parameters, the CMB rest frame, Huygens&#8217; Principle, the Principle of Least Action, Noether&#8217;s theorem and the full catalog of Poincaré-group conservation laws, the holographic principle, AdS/CFT, the Kaluza–Klein framework, Penrose&#8217;s twistor theory, loop quantum gravity, the amplituhedron, and the resolution of Jacobson&#8217;s thermodynamic-spacetime and Verlinde&#8217;s entropic-gravity programs.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The specific contents of the derivation catalog follow.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>From quantum mechanics:</strong></p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>The Schrödinger equation (non-relativistic limit) — derived from the McGucken Principle as the rate of x₄-phase accumulation for matter at sub-luminal velocity [MG-Schrödinger references, in MG-Proof].</li>



<li>The Dirac equation (relativistic) with its Clifford algebra, spin-½, SU(2) double cover, and matter-antimatter structure — derived in [MG-Dirac, §§II–VIII].</li>



<li>The de Broglie relation p = h/λ — derived in [MG-deBroglie] from the geometric relation between x₄-phase accumulation and spatial momentum.</li>



<li>The Heisenberg uncertainty principle — derived as a consequence of the finite Planck-wavelength structure of x₄&#8217;s oscillation.</li>



<li>The Born rule for probability amplitudes — derived in [MG-Born] from x₄&#8217;s oscillatory geometry.</li>



<li>Quantum nonlocality and the EPR correlations — derived in [MG-Nonlocality] as consequences of x₄&#8217;s spherically-symmetric expansion from a common generating event.</li>



<li>The Copenhagen interpretation&#8217;s measurement structure — derived in [MG-Copenhagen] as the projection of x₄-oscillation onto a measurement apparatus.</li>



<li>Wave-particle duality, dissolved as an ontological puzzle — [MG-deBroglie, §VI] shows that &#8220;wave&#8221; and &#8220;particle&#8221; are pre-localization and post-localization aspects of the single expanding McGucken Sphere.</li>



<li>The QED Lagrangian and gauge structure — derived in [MG-QED] from the U(1) gauge invariance of x₄&#8217;s phase.</li>



<li>The commutation relations [x, p] = iℏ — derived in [MG-Commut] from the oscillation structure of x₄.</li>



<li>The path integral formalism and Wick rotation — derived in [MG-PathInt] and [MG-Wick] from the analytic-continuation structure of x₄ = ict.</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>From relativity and gravity:</strong></p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Minkowski signature η = diag(−1, +1, +1, +1) — derived from the i in dx₄/dt = ic.</li>



<li>Special relativity, Lorentz invariance, and the invariance of c — derived from the master equation u^μu_μ = −c² that follows from the McGucken Principle [MG-Proof].</li>



<li>Time dilation and the twins paradox — derived geometrically as consequences of x₄-advance at different rates in different frames.</li>



<li>The Schwarzschild metric and gravitational time dilation — derived in [MG-GR] from x₄-advance coupling to spatial curvature.</li>



<li>The Einstein field equations G_{μν} = (8πG/c⁴) T_{μν} — derived in [MG-GR] as the ADM foliation of spacetime by constant-x₄ hypersurfaces.</li>



<li>Gravitational waves as spatial-metric undulations with x₄ as invariant carrier — [MG-GR].</li>



<li>Black holes as regions where spatial curvature prevents further x₄-advance — [MG-GR].</li>



<li>Newton&#8217;s law of universal gravitation as the non-relativistic limit — [MG-GR].</li>



<li>The ADM formalism and the semiclassical limit — [MG-GR].</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>From thermodynamics and the arrow of time:</strong></p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>The second law of thermodynamics as a geometric necessity rather than a statistical tendency — derived in [MG-Jacobson, §III] from x₄&#8217;s spherically symmetric expansion dragging particles isotropically, producing the diffusion equation ∂P/∂t = D∇²P with D = c²δt/6 and the monotonic entropy growth dS/dt = (3/2)k_B/t &gt; 0 for all t &gt; 0. Entropy cannot decrease because x₄ cannot retreat; there is no Poincaré recurrence because x₄&#8217;s advance is irreversible.</li>



<li>The thermodynamic arrow of time — derived as the sign-convention consequence of dx₄/dt = +ic, not −ic [MG-Broken].</li>



<li>The unification of seven arrows of time (thermodynamic, cosmological, psychological, quantum measurement, electromagnetic, weak-force, gravitational) — all shown to have the single source dx₄/dt = +ic [MG-Broken, MG-Eleven, MG-Jacobson §III.4].</li>



<li>Jacobson&#8217;s thermodynamic derivation of Einstein&#8217;s equations (1995) — [MG-Jacobson, §III.3] supplies the microscopic mechanism Jacobson identified as missing: x₄&#8217;s irreversible spherically symmetric expansion is the physical process that makes the Clausius relation δQ = TdS hold at every local Rindler horizon, with the entropy-area proportionality S = A/(4ℓ_P²) arising because each Planck-area cell on the horizon accommodates one quantum of x₄&#8217;s expansion direction.</li>



<li>Verlinde&#8217;s entropic gravity (2010, 2016) — [MG-Verlinde] identifies Verlinde&#8217;s holographic screen with the McGucken Sphere — the surface at radius R = ct of x₄&#8217;s spherically symmetric expansion from any event. The information content N = A/λ_P² follows from the quantization of x₄&#8217;s oscillation at Planck wavelength (one quantum per Planck-area cell on the sphere surface, rather than being an independent consequence of the holographic principle). Verlinde&#8217;s entropy-change formula ΔS = 2πk_B mcΔx/ℏ is derived from the Gaussian distribution of x₄&#8217;s spherically symmetric Brownian expansion evaluated at the McGucken Sphere radius. The Unruh temperature T = ℏa/(2πck_B) emerges as the temperature of x₄&#8217;s oscillatory expansion perceived by an accelerating observer whose x₄-advance rate is modulated by their acceleration. Newton&#8217;s law F = GMm/R², Einstein&#8217;s field equations (via the Clausius relation δQ = TδS applied to x₄&#8217;s expansion through the McGucken Sphere), the volume-law entropy contribution of Verlinde&#8217;s 2016 extension (baseline entropy density of x₄&#8217;s zero-point Planck-scale oscillation), and the baryonic Tully–Fisher relation v⁴ = GM·cH₀/(2π) all follow from x₄&#8217;s isotropic expansion producing the entropy, the screen, and the force. Verlinde&#8217;s 2010 paper provided the thermodynamic derivation; [MG-Verlinde] supplies the physical mechanism — x₄&#8217;s spherical expansion — that Verlinde&#8217;s framework requires but left unspecified.</li>



<li>Marolf&#8217;s nonlocality constraint (2014) — that emergent gravity requires kinematic nonlocality — is satisfied by the McGucken framework through three mechanisms treated in [MG-Jacobson, §V]: (a) the global invariance of dx₄/dt = ic (a single globally-defined expansion rate, not a field of independent local values), (b) the McGucken-Equivalence structure of entanglement (particles that share a common origin share a common x₄ coordinate because null intervals preserve x₄-locality), and (c) the boundary-Hamiltonian property induced by x₄&#8217;s invariant advance making total energy a boundary rather than a bulk quantity.</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>From the Standard Model:</strong></p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>The gauge group structure SU(3) × SU(2) × U(1) — derived in [MG-SM, MG-Noether] from x₄&#8217;s phase invariance and the transverse/parallel spatial-triple structure.</li>



<li>Maxwell&#8217;s equations — derived in [MG-SM] from U(1) local x₄-phase invariance.</li>



<li>The Yang–Mills equations for non-Abelian gauge theories — derived in [MG-SM, MG-Noether].</li>

<li>Spontaneous chiral symmetry breaking in QCD — [MG-Broken].</li>



<li>Parity (P), charge-conjugation (C), CP, and time-reversal (T) violation patterns — all derived in [MG-Broken] from the directed expansion +ic (not −ic).</li>

<li>The three Sakharov conditions for baryogenesis — [MG-Broken].</li>



<li>The strong CP problem&#8217;s resolution — [MG-Broken].</li>

<li>Integer charge quantization as a geometric theorem — [MG-Noether, Proposition VI.8].</li>



<li>Absence of magnetic monopoles as a bundle-triviality theorem — [MG-Noether, Proposition VI.10].</li>



<li>The non-existence of the graviton as a particle degree of freedom, because gravity is not a force but an emergent geometric consequence of x₄-dynamics — [MG-GR, MG-Dirac §X.3].</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>From cosmology:</strong></p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>The CMB rest frame and its identification with absolute rest in the spatial triple — [MG-Mech-CMB].</li>



<li>The cosmological constant and dark energy equation of state w_eff(z) = −1 + Ω_m(z)/(6π) as a derived functional form with no free parameters — [MG-Lambda].</li>



<li>The Hubble expansion as the cosmological-scale projection of x₄-advance — [MG-Mech].</li>



<li>The &#8220;Axis of Evil&#8221; anomaly in the CMB — interpreted as a consequence of the specific x₄-advance orientation [MG-Mech-CMB].</li>



<li>The low-entropy initial conditions problem (Past Hypothesis) — dissolved in [MG-Eleven] as a consequence of x₄&#8217;s advance starting from a maximally-coherent initial state.</li>



<li>The time-dependent diffusion constant D(t) ∝ t^m and its connection to dark-energy w_eff — [MG-Lambda].</li>



<li>The resolution of eleven cosmological mysteries from the single principle — enumerated in [MG-Eleven].</li>



<li>Cosmological holography on FRW and de Sitter space — [MG-FRW-Holography].</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>From other unified frameworks:</strong></p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>The Kaluza–Klein framework, completed — [MG-KaluzaKlein] identifies the Kaluza–Klein fifth dimension as x₄ itself and shows that the five-dimensional formalism reduces to the McGucken four-dimensional Minkowski framework, resolving the century-long question of what the Kaluza–Klein fifth dimension physically is.</li>



<li>The holographic principle and AdS/CFT — [MG-Holography] identifies dx₄/dt = ic as the geometric source of boundary-bulk duality.</li>



<li>Penrose&#8217;s twistor theory — [MG-Eleven] shows that the complex-null structure of twistor geometry arises from x₄&#8217;s complex-valued advance.</li>



<li>Loop Quantum Gravity — a separate paper (referenced in the corpus) shows that the spin-network combinatorial structure of LQG corresponds to the discretized x₄-oscillation-cycle structure of the McGucken framework.</li>



<li>String theory — in the present paper and its companions, the full duality web of the five superstring theories plus 11D supergravity.</li>



<li>The Amplituhedron — [MG-Amplituhedron] identifies the amplituhedron as the canonical-form shadow of x₄&#8217;s expansion, with the emergent-locality and emergent-unitarity features following from x₄&#8217;s geometric structure.</li>



<li>Jacobson&#8217;s thermodynamic spacetime and Marolf&#8217;s nonlocality constraint — [MG-Jacobson].</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>From mathematical physics:</strong></p>



<ul class="wp-block-list">
<li>Huygens&#8217; Principle — derived as the geometric content of x₄&#8217;s spherically symmetric advance from every event.</li>



<li>The Principle of Least Action — derived from the observation that S = −mc ∫|dx₄| is the unique Lorentz-scalar reparametrization-invariant functional of the worldline [MG-Noether, Proposition II.10].</li>



<li>Noether&#8217;s theorem and the full catalog of Poincaré-group conservation laws (energy, three momenta, three angular momenta, three boost charges) — [MG-Noether, §§IV–V].</li>



<li>The complexification of physics — the twelve instances where the imaginary unit i appears &#8220;by hand&#8221; in quantum theory, all traced to the single i of dx₄/dt = ic — [MG-Noether, §VII.5].</li>
</ul>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>The simplicity gap.</strong> Witten&#8217;s framework requires ten- or eleven-dimensional target spaces, five perturbatively-distinct string theories, a 10^500-vacuum landscape, a duality web connecting six frameworks, and a non-perturbative theory (M-theory) whose fundamental formulation remains unknown after thirty years. The McGucken framework requires one equation, dx₄/dt = ic. All of Witten&#8217;s results are theorems of the McGucken equation, and the McGucken equation additionally generates the Schrödinger equation, the Dirac equation, the Standard Model, general relativity, thermodynamic irreversibility, the cosmological constant, and the major alternative frameworks (holography, twistor theory, loop quantum gravity, the amplituhedron). This is the Einstein standard — as simple as possible, not simpler — applied to foundational physics: dx₄/dt = ic is the simplest principle from which the known content of fundamental physics has been shown to follow, and no simpler principle of comparable reach is currently known.</p>



<h3 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>X.4.8 Objection B2: &#8220;The McGucken framework uses structural arguments rather than calculations. How do I verify its claims?&#8221;</strong></h3>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>The objection.</strong> Much of the present paper consists of structural identifications — &#8220;both descriptions parameterize the same x₄-flux data,&#8221; &#8220;the McGucken framework supplies the geometric reason.&#8221; A reviewer will ask: how do I verify these claims computationally?</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>The McGucken Framework&#8217;s Response.</strong> The present paper contains three explicit worked calculations: the quintic Calabi–Yau dimension count (§II.6.1.k), the T-duality spectrum match on S¹ (§V.3), and the BPS matching on K3 via the Narain isomorphism (§VI.3). These are verifiable by direct computation. Further verification is available through:</p>



<ol class="wp-block-list">
<li><strong>The full LTD corpus derivations.</strong> Each of the results listed in §X.4.7 is derived in its respective corpus paper with explicit calculation. A reviewer who wants to verify the framework&#8217;s claims about (say) the Dirac equation can check the derivation in [MG-Dirac]; for the Schwarzschild metric, [MG-GR]; for entropy increase, [MG-Jacobson]; and so on.</li>



<li><strong>The matching with known results.</strong> Where the McGucken framework claims to reproduce a standard-framework result, the match is explicit and computationally verifiable. The quintic&#8217;s h^{1,1} = 1, h^{2,1} = 101 Hodge numbers; the 480-state count at M² = 1 on the heterotic/Type IIA K3 sector; the T-duality spectrum match at every mass level; the Einstein equations as the σ-model beta-function zero — all these are computations, and the framework&#8217;s claim is that the computations yield the same answers in both languages.</li>



<li><strong>Absolute predictions.</strong> The framework makes specific falsifiable predictions: no magnetic monopoles (bundle-triviality theorem, [MG-Noether Proposition VI.10]), exact photon masslessness ([MG-Noether, Proposition VI.9]), integer charge quantization ([MG-Noether, Proposition VI.8]), absence of the spin-2 graviton ([MG-GR]), exact CPT ([MG-Dirac, §VIII.9]), dark-energy equation of state w(z) = −1 + Ω_m(z)/(6π) with no free parameters ([MG-Lambda]). Any of these can be checked against experiment, and any single violation would refute the framework at the foundational level.</li>
</ol>



<h3 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>X.4.9 A Vast Foundational Physical Unification</strong></h3>



<blockquote class="wp-block-quote is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><em>&#8220;Behind it all is surely an idea so simple, so beautiful, that when we grasp it — in a decade, a century, or a millennium — we will all say to each other, how could it have been otherwise?&#8221;</em> — John Archibald Wheeler</p>
</blockquote>



<blockquote class="wp-block-quote is-layout-flow wp-block-quote-is-layout-flow">
<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><em>&#8220;Everything should be made as simple as possible, but not simpler.&#8221;</em> — Albert Einstein</p>
</blockquote>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The McGucken Principle identifies a single physical structure — x₄, a real geometric axis advancing at rate ic — as the common source of a vast range of fundamental physical results. The derivation catalog of §X.4.7 lists them: the Schrödinger equation, the Dirac equation with its Clifford algebra and spin-½ structure, the Minkowski signature, the Lorentz invariance of c, the Schwarzschild metric, the Einstein field equations, the Standard Model gauge group SU(3) × SU(2) × U(1), Maxwell&#8217;s equations, the Higgs mechanism, CP and T violation, CPT exactness, the second law of thermodynamics and the seven arrows of time, the Hubble expansion, the cosmological constant, the CMB rest frame, Huygens&#8217; principle, the Principle of Least Action, Noether&#8217;s theorem, the holographic principle, AdS/CFT, the Kaluza–Klein framework, twistor theory, loop quantum gravity, string theory, M-theory and its duality web, the amplituhedron. Each derivation is worked out in its corpus paper; the paper containing the derivation is cited in the catalog; each derivation is checkable.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The framework also generates specific absolute predictions that the string frameworks do not force. Magnetic monopoles cannot exist — a bundle-triviality theorem from the global +ic directionality of x₄-expansion ([MG-Noether, Proposition VI.10]). The photon is exactly massless — a geometric theorem ([MG-Noether, Proposition VI.9]). Electric charge is quantized in integer units — a theorem ([MG-Noether, Proposition VI.8]). The spin-2 graviton does not exist as a particle degree of freedom; gravity is an emergent geometric consequence of x₄-dynamics ([MG-GR]). CPT is exact, as the automatic consequence of full 4D geometric inversion ([MG-Dirac, §VIII.9]). The dark-energy equation of state is w(z) = −1 + Ω_m(z)/(6π) with no free parameters ([MG-Lambda]). Each prediction is falsifiable, and any single violation refutes the framework at the foundational level.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The scope of this unification — quantum mechanics, special and general relativity, the Standard Model, thermodynamics, cosmology, and the major frameworks of theoretical physics — exceeds the scope of every currently-established unification in fundamental physics. Newton unified terrestrial and celestial motion. Maxwell unified electricity, magnetism, and optics. The Standard Model unified the three non-gravitational forces. String theory and M-theory unify the five superstring theories and eleven-dimensional supergravity. The McGucken Principle unifies all of these and their foundations under the single geometric statement dx₄/dt = ic.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The physical structure identified — x₄ as a real advancing axis — is the one Minkowski wrote in 1908 and the one Witten rediscovered at strong coupling in 1995. The derivations collected in §X.4.7 are the consequences of recognizing this axis as physical rather than notational.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The Principle meets both the Wheeler standard and the Einstein standard. It is simple: one equation, dx₄/dt = ic. It is not simpler than possible: the complete range of fundamental physics listed above follows from it by derivation, not by postulate, and no equation simpler than dx₄/dt = ic generates a comparable derivational reach. This is the form Wheeler expected the answer to take, and the form Einstein held as the criterion for a successful unification.</p>



<h1 class="wp-block-heading"><strong>XI. Conclusion: M Stands for McGucken</strong></h1>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">For thirty years, M-theory has existed in a curious position within theoretical physics. It is universally accepted as the unifying framework for the five perturbative superstring theories and eleven-dimensional supergravity — Witten 1995&#8217;s identification of this unification is one of the most influential results of late-twentieth-century physics, and the duality-web picture has been confirmed, extended, and applied with extraordinary success [16, 17, 18]. Yet M-theory itself has remained without a fundamental formulation. It is known only through its limits: 11D supergravity at low energy, Type IIA at small g_s, Type IIB at its own weak coupling, heterotic E₈ × E₈ on the Hořava–Witten orbifold, and so on. Nathan Seiberg has called this situation &#8220;unsatisfactory&#8221; [11]. Juan Maldacena has said that &#8220;we don&#8217;t know what M-theory is&#8221; [19].</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">The McGucken Principle identifies what M-theory is. M-theory is the theory of x₄&#8217;s oscillatory advance: dx₄/dt = ic, with the oscillation at the Planck wavelength (Proposition II.1), generating string-like behavior without requiring additional spatial dimensions beyond x₄ (Proposition II.2), and with its compactifications giving the Kaluza–Klein spectrum of 11D supergravity (Proposition IV.1). The five perturbative superstring theories and 11D supergravity are six perturbative expansions of x₄&#8217;s Huygens cascade around six different classical backgrounds, connected by dualities that are automorphism groups of the x₄-flux lattice on compactification manifolds (Propositions V.1, VI.1, VII.1, IX.1). Witten&#8217;s identification in 1995 that the strong-coupling limit of Type IIA is eleven-dimensional was the discovery that x₄ is visible as a geometric dimension when g_s is large (Propositions III.1, III.2).</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Three structural observations frame the conclusion.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">First, the eleventh dimension that Witten identified in 1995 is not new. It is x₄, the real geometric fourth axis of Minkowski spacetime, present since Minkowski&#8217;s 1908 formulation [13] and read for a century as notational convenience [MG-Commut, §1]. The McGucken Principle identifies what Minkowski&#8217;s notation was pointing at: a physical process — x₄&#8217;s advance at rate ic — that underlies all of modern physics. Witten saw x₄ in 1995 by looking at the strong-coupling limit of Type IIA string theory. Minkowski wrote x₄ = ict in 1908. The two discoveries are of the same physical axis, read in different frameworks.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Second, the duality web connecting the five perturbative string theories and 11D supergravity is not a coincidence of six different theories happening to have compatible low-energy limits. It is the consequence of a single underlying geometric fact: all six frameworks are perturbative descriptions of the same x₄-oscillatory advance, and their dualities are the isomorphisms between different parametrizations of the same underlying flux data. Witten&#8217;s duality arguments — BPS spectrum matching, moduli-space agreement, effective-action coincidence — are valid not because of six independent coincidences but because of a single structural fact.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Third, the non-perturbative formulation of M-theory that has eluded the duality program since 1995 is the McGucken Principle itself. dx₄/dt = ic is a complete, non-perturbative, geometric specification of the fundamental physical theory. It is not a perturbative expansion around a classical background; it is a direct statement about the geometry of spacetime. All perturbative string theories, all supergravity formulations, all duality relations, all BPS states, all D-branes are derivations from this one geometric statement combined with the classical backgrounds and boundary conditions of each perturbative frame.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>The five perturbative superstring theories are five perturbative expansions of x₄&#8217;s oscillatory advance.</strong></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>Eleven-dimensional supergravity is the low-energy limit of x₄&#8217;s advance.</strong></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>The duality web connecting them is the automorphism group of the x₄-flux lattice on each compactification.</strong></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>M-theory is the theory of x₄&#8217;s advance.</strong></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><strong>dx₄/dt = ic is M-theory.</strong></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">Witten wrote in 1995 that &#8220;M stands for magic, mystery, or membrane, according to taste&#8221; [5, 9]. Thirty years later, the McGucken Principle suggests an additional reading: M stands for McGucken. The theory Witten named in 1995 and whose perturbative limits he catalogued is the theory of x₄&#8217;s advance. The naming preceded the identification; the identification has now been made.</p>

McGucken, &#8220;The Amplituhedron from dx₄/dt = ic: Positive Geometry, Emergent Locality and Unitarity, Dual Conformal Symmetry, the Yangian, and the Absence of Spacetime as Theorems of the McGucken Principle of McGucken&#8217;s Fourth Expanding Dimension,&#8221; Light Time Dimension Theory (April 2026). Companion paper identifying the amplituhedron as the canonical-form shadow of x₄&#8217;s expansion.</h2>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph"><em>Primary historical sources for the McGucken Principle, cited in the Historical Note:</em></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[Wheeler-Letter] J. A. Wheeler, Letter of recommendation for Elliot McGucken, Princeton University, Joseph Henry Professor of Physics (c. 1990). Quoted in full in the Historical Note above.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[MG-Dissertation] E. McGucken, <em>Multiple Unit Artificial Retina Chipset to Aid the Visually Impaired and Enhanced Holed-Emitter CMOS Phototransistors</em>. NSF-funded Ph.D. dissertation, University of North Carolina at Chapel Hill (1998). Appendix contains the first written formulation of the McGucken Principle, treating time as an emergent phenomenon arising from a fourth expanding dimension.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[MG-FQXi2008] E. McGucken, &#8220;Time as an Emergent Phenomenon: Traveling Back to the Heroic Age of Physics (In Memory of John Archibald Wheeler),&#8221; Foundational Questions Institute essay (August 2008). First formal treatment of the McGucken Principle in the scholarly literature. <a href="https://forums.fqxi.org/d/238-time-as-an-emergent-phenomenon-traveling-back-to-the-heroic-age-of-physics-by-elliot-mcgucken">https://forums.fqxi.org/d/238-time-as-an-emergent-phenomenon-traveling-back-to-the-heroic-age-of-physics-by-elliot-mcgucken</a></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[MG-FQXi2009] E. McGucken, &#8220;What is Ultimately Possible in Physics?,&#8221; Foundational Questions Institute essay (2009). <a href="https://forums.fqxi.org/d/432">https://forums.fqxi.org/d/432</a></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[MG-FQXi2011] E. McGucken, &#8220;On the Emergence of QM, Relativity, Entropy, Time, iℏ, and ic from the Foundational, Physical Reality of a Fourth Dimension x₄ Expanding with a Discrete (Digital) Wavelength λ_P at c Relative to Three Continuous (Analog) Spatial Dimensions,&#8221; Foundational Questions Institute essay (2010–2011). First explicit identification of the structural parallel between dx₄/dt = ic and the canonical commutation relation [q, p] = iℏ.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[MG-FQXi2012] E. McGucken, &#8220;MDT&#8217;s dx₄/dt = ic Triumphs Over the Wrong Physical Assumption that Time is a Dimension,&#8221; Foundational Questions Institute essay (2012). <a href="https://forums.fqxi.org/d/1429">https://forums.fqxi.org/d/1429</a></p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[MG-FQXi2013] E. McGucken, &#8220;Where is the Wisdom we have lost in Information?,&#8221; Foundational Questions Institute essay (2013).</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[MG-Book2016] E. McGucken, <em>Light Time Dimension Theory: The Foundational Physics Unifying Einstein&#8217;s Relativity and Quantum Mechanics. A Simple, Illustrated Introduction to the Physical Model of the Fourth Expanding Dimension</em>. 45EPIC Hero&#8217;s Odyssey Mythology Press (2016). Amazon ASIN: B01KP8XGQ6.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[MG-BookTime] E. McGucken, <em>The Physics of Time: Time and Its Arrows in Quantum Mechanics, Relativity, the Second Law of Thermodynamics, Entropy, the Twin Paradox, and Cosmology Explained via LTD Theory&#8217;s Expanding Fourth Dimension</em>. 45EPIC Hero&#8217;s Odyssey Mythology Press (2017). Amazon ASIN: B0F2PZCW6B.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[MG-BookEntanglement] E. McGucken, <em>Quantum Entanglement &amp; Einstein&#8217;s Spooky Action at a Distance Explained: The Foundational Physics of Quantum Mechanics&#8217; Nonlocality &amp; Probability: The Nonlocality of the Fourth Expanding Dimension</em>. 45EPIC Hero&#8217;s Odyssey Mythology Press (2017). Contains the Peebles-exchange passage quoted in the Historical Note.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[MG-BookRelativity] E. McGucken, <em>Einstein&#8217;s Relativity Derived from LTD Theory&#8217;s Principle</em>. 45EPIC Hero&#8217;s Odyssey Mythology Press (2017).</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[MG-BookTriumph] E. McGucken, <em>The Triumph of LTD Theory and Physics over String Theory, the Multiverse, Inflation, Supersymmetry, M-Theory, LQG, and Failed Pseudoscience: How dx₄/dt = ic Unifies Physics</em>. 45EPIC Hero&#8217;s Odyssey Mythology Press (2017). Amazon ASIN: B01N19KO3A. First book-length articulation of the thesis formally proved in Proposition II.5 of the present paper: the extra spatial dimensions of string theory and M-theory are not physically required.</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[MG-BookPictures] E. McGucken, <em>Relativity and Quantum Mechanics Unified in Pictures: A Simple, Intuitive, Illustrated Introduction to LTD Theory&#8217;s Unification of Einstein&#8217;s Relativity</em>. 45EPIC Hero&#8217;s Odyssey Mythology Press (2017).</p>



<p class="wp-block-paragraph">[MG-BookHero] E. McGucken, Hero&#8217;s Odyssey Mythology Physics series (additional volume). 45EPIC Hero&#8217;s Odyssey Mythology Press (2017).</p>
